Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Last weekend, the **Magic Online** Live Series traveled to PAX East, the Penny Arcade Expo in Boston, where winner Marilith bested cowwithhat in the Finals of a Top 8 featuring triple *Urza's Saga* draft. *Urza's Saga* is now available in the **Magic Online** store, so it's a fine time to catch up (or brush up) on the draft format!

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/act418_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20100331000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=1073813)

## top 8 bracket

### Quaterfinals

**Marilith**

superpriest

**Seeker**

Diakonov

**cowwithhat**

cheetonian

### Semi-finals

**Marilith, 2-1**

Seeker, 2-0

**cowwithhat, 2-1**

mabgot7, 2-1

### Finals

**Marilith, 2-0**

cowwithhat, 2-1

### Champion

**Marilith, 2-0**

  

![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/da418_players.jpg)*Finalist cowwithhat, left, and winner Marilith*
